Today's Analysis

Odds 8:23 AM PT

Top TD Players Updated Oct 25, 8:29 AM PT

Detroit (29.5 implied) and the 49ers (26.3) carry the biggest team totals before 5 PM PT, so Jahmyr Gibbs (#0), Christian McCaffrey (#23) and Bijan Robinson (#7) top every tier. Absences push work to a few backs: David Montgomery is out for Houston (Woody Marks (#4) leads), Mike Evans and Deebo Samuel Sr. are out for San Francisco, and Lamar Jackson is out for Baltimore with Tyler Huntley (#5) starting.

  • 10 games kicking off between 10:00 AM and 1:25 PM PT. Steelers @ Saints (London, 6:32 AM PT) was already in progress when this was written and is left out, as is Chiefs @ Seahawks (SNF, 5:20 PM PT).
  • Friday injury report (nflverse): Lamar Jackson (#8, BAL) out, so Tyler Huntley (#5) starts. David Montgomery (HOU) out, which leaves Woody Marks (#4) as lead back. For SF, Mike Evans, Deebo Samuel Sr. and Nick Bosa are out, and Christian Kirk is questionable. Emeka Egbuka (TB) is out with a concussion. Jeremiyah Love (ARI) is out, and Carson Beck (ARI) is questionable, with Jacoby Brissett (#7) quoted as the starter. Tyler Warren (IND) is out.
  • Questionable starters who could change the list: Puka Nacua (#12, LAR, calf, did not practice Friday), Breece Hall (#20, NYJ, quad), Jordan Mason (#27, MIN, thumb), Baker Mayfield (#6, TB, thumb, full practice).
  • Searches for 2026 Week 8 weather returned only past seasons. No wind or rain reports were checked for the outdoor games.
  • Method: de-vigged 1+ prices (two-sided at Pinnacle/NoVig) across 10 books, adjusted a few points for red-zone share, team totals and injuries. 2+/3+ come from the adjusted 1+ rate (Poisson), with a small bump for goal-line backs on big totals.

The shared script is unchanged: Kenneth Walker III (#9) on the ground for KC and Jaxon Smith-Njigba (#11) through the air for Seattle.

RB Brashard Smith went on IR Saturday. CB L'Jarius Sneed (knee) is active. LB Cooper McDonald is active for the first time since Week 1. TE Jared Wiley is active for the first time this season, which could take a few snaps from Travis Kelce (#87). Kelce is active.
    • SEA inactives (final): RB Zach Charbonnet, RB Robert Henry Jr., LBs Drake Thomas, Chazz Surratt and Connor O'Toole, and QB Drew Lock (#2, emergency third QB). One outlet also lists G Beau Stephens as inactive, but that is unconfirmed.

The spread is still −3, but Pinnacle now has Seattle −3 at −101 / KC +3 at −109, which is 51.9% Seattle no-vig (it was 49.5%). ML: Pinnacle no-vig Seattle 58.4%. Walker's rush line went from 79.5 to 80.5 at three books. The 95.5 line for Smith-Njigba is gone; he sits at 91.5 or 97.5. Kelce went from 44.5 to 45.5.

    Parlay: The Over correlates with the Smith-Njigba, Kelce and Walker Overs (a game with points). Seahawks −2.5 pairs with Smith-Njigba and Wilson but works against the Kansas City legs, so keep it out of a same-game parlay with Walker or Kelce. A same-game parlay must be built at one book, which prices the correlation itself. Its vig compounds, so the parlay gives up most of any single-leg edge.
